Rules the brief must hold:
- **The page (9.4).** Heading "Checks of retrain" (the posterior in use), "Checks before training on retrain_prior" (a prior, no posterior), "Checks" (nothing); sentence "A posterior passes when all six deciding checks have passed." or, for a posterior trained without the frequency response, "A posterior passes when the three deciding checks that apply to it have passed."; "In use: posterior retrain, trained on prior retrain_prior" (W022); then the headline, the page's only line at heading size (the look's heading type, as `PageHead` sets its title), its sentence under it; then the listing. The listing is one ruled table, columns "Check", "Role", "State", "Last run", the four group headings as heading rows spanning the table, each check a row that is a stop and opens its page by a click, Space or Enter; with the narrow listing (beside a detail) only "Check" and "State", a state word wrapping rather than cut. `CHECK_BESIDE_WIDTH = 890` (scaled; probe 5's two columns beside a page at text 1.0), handed to `ListAndDetail`.
- **Which check is open.** None at a launch: the whole table. A row opens its `CheckPage`; `open_check(key)` hands that page's form to the foot (`use_form`). With no check open the foot's main button reads "Open " + the name of the first deciding check that applies and has not passed ("Open Calibration"), or "Go to Inference" when every one that applies has, and opens it; its facts are none; greyed with "Needs a prior: build or load one in step 2, Prior." when nothing can be checked. The twelve `CheckPage`s are built in the constructor, which reads nothing. One lock over all twelve forms: `ValidatePanel.set_controls_enabled(on)` locks and unlocks every check page's form, not only the one in sight, and `use_form` gives the form it hands over the page's present lock, so a check opened during a run shows a locked form and every form is enabled when the run ends. The list's facts and each check's "Before you start" say the earlier steps' changes not applied (`_others_pending`, steps 1 to 3), a warning, never a block.
- **A check's page (9.4).** Top to bottom: the name, the sentence of 9.1, the state line (the row's line: "Passed, 12 Oct 2026, 14:05", "Not run yet", "Waiting for a posterior"), the result block of the record that counts (or of the newest entry, for the timing and the card test), "Earlier runs" (one line each, newest first: "Failed, 11 Oct 2026, 09:30"; a reduced one "Run at a reduced size, 10 Oct 2026, 15:00; does not count"), then the check's form. The foot holds the check's "Before you start" and its main button, the check's `button` words or "Run again" once a record exists; check 3 has none: its foot's button is the listing's. The button is greyed with 9.4's reasons, each a `Fact("lock", <reason>, <blocks>)`: the live-run sentence of 5.6; "Needs a posterior: train one in step 3, or load a saved one." (4, 5, 6, 9, 12); "The prior in use is not the one this posterior was trained on (retrain_prior). Load it in step 2." (4, 9, when `Held.pair_refused` is not ""; departure 408); "This posterior was trained without keeping its simulations, and this check reads them." (5); "This posterior was trained without the rotation, so there are no directions to read." (6); "Applies only with the frequency response." (1, 2, 3, and 9 with a fixed number); "Applies only to the Nadrowski model." (7); "Needs a bounds file with a forcing section." (11, 12, and 7 without the frequency response); "Does not apply with the frequency response." (12). Their leads (the head's line on the stage-4 exit's answers): "Cannot start yet:" for the live run, "Needs a posterior…" and the prior pair (a load lifts them); "Cannot start:" for the two "trained without" reasons and the four that a model, a kind of observation or a bounds file decides, each joining `step_facts`' table of blocks that waiting will not lift. Until Tasks 6 to 8 build a check's form, its button is greyed with `NOT_BUILT_TEXT = "This check cannot be run from the window yet."` (departure 423), but check 4's, which keeps today's two rows ("Test datasets" `n_cal`, "Operating points" `cal_n_scales`, read by `require_at_least` at the press, with `help_text=HELP["cal_n"]` and `HELP["cal_scales"]`, `check_forms.py` importing `from .help_text import HELP`, so the help entries keep a reader under `core/gui/panels`) and today's call, dispatched by its function's name (`self.dispatch(orchestrator.validate_calibration, …)`), with the seed drawn by `random.randrange(2**31)` and named in its result.
- **Reading (9.4, 9.5).** Everything a check's page, a form's "Before you start" or a result block shows is read in `read_checks` or the page's `read_disk` and held, never in `facts_from_form`, `refresh_local_gates` or `result_block` (which `refresh_gates` reaches while a run is live, through `take_posterior` and `take_observation`). `read_disk()` calls `screen.read_checks()` and draws from `checks_held`; triggers as R-check-reading. While a run is live the page shows what it read last, and check 3 shows the live training's comparison from memory, `controller().model.comparison()` and the run's answers (once Task 9 builds them; until then "Counted when simulation ends"), never the history file. Check 3 afterwards: `Held.set_counts` (peek, else the log); for a posterior from a narrowing round the page adds "The audit measured the whole prior; this round simulated inside a narrowed region."
- **The result blocks (9.4's "The result"),** one per key, from a manifest and its folder (or a history entry for `timing` and `card_test`), drawn into a `ResultRegion`: the verdict sentence (a message row, its marker the state's, beginning with the check's name), the table, the pictures. The six deciding sentences are 9.4's examples word for word for "Passed"; "Failed" and "Look closer" keep their first clause's form ("Calibration: failed. 2 of 13 parameters fail the rank test: T, D; the joint coverage test passes. Made on …"); check 6's sentence drops "about 4.3 effectively pinned down", which no field holds (departure 419): "Identifiability of the posterior: passed. The record holds how strongly each of the 13 directions is pinned down: the strongest 1.2e6 times the weakest. This check passes when those figures are on record; the table says what they are." The informing checks and tools read "done": "What the observations can pin down: done. At 4.5 s, 2 parameters have no local information and 1 pair moves an observation alike." (above its table, from the record's log read by its folder in `read_checks` (`check_lineage.read_text`, held with the record), "Driven recordings at the low end of the band hold two cycles from 3.1 s." with the frequency response only); "Calibration repeated at fixed numbers of driven recordings: done. With 4 driven recordings, 7 of 10 repeats pass the rank test. This is the rank half only; the joint coverage test is Calibration's." (one line per variant); "Drive study: done. The cell keeps its own oscillation up to a drive of 0.8 N and is captured from 2.5 N."; "Identifiability across the prior: done, at 6 points across the prior. The table gives each parameter's spread at each point and the share of points where recordings pin it down." The tables are 9.4's, their columns' words the window's, never the record's own strings. `FIGURE_CAPTIONS` keys the calibration's three by `store.slug` of the engine's titles ("SBC ranks (CDF)" → "Rank test, cumulative"; "SBC ranks (histogram)" → "Rank test, histogram"; "TARP coverage" → "Joint coverage test"); a figure it does not name is captioned "<check name>, figure N".
- **What is never shown (9.9),** each a test: no heading-size, boxed or coloured "PASS" or "Passed" for one check; no sentence that a posterior passed below every deciding check that applies; under a passed calibration with others open, "Calibration alone does not show that the posterior is sound: 2 deciding checks are still open."; no "Passed" or "Failed" for an informing check, a tool or a reduced record; no pass for check 6 without its clause, none for check 3 after an acceptance without its words; the engine's `[verdict] PASS` only under "Show the full text".
- **The calibration's run (row 99).** `RunSpec("Check", ("inference", "checks"), name="Calibration", stop_button="Stop the calibration…", kinds=("calibration",), plan=screen.plan_entry(), check={"key": "calibration", "sizes": {"n_cal": …, "cal_n_scales": …}})`: a check's run hands `plan` (the plan in use, as Training's does) and no `settings`, its sizes being in its own record, or in `check.sizes` for the two that keep none. Its stop question stays today's `questions._OTHERS["Check"]`: title "Stop the calibration?", text "A check writes its record only when it finishes, so nothing of this one is kept.", answers "Keep it running" (preselected) and "Stop and discard it" (destructive). Its end: "The calibration finished." / "The calibration was stopped; nothing was kept." / 6.9's failure sentence. Another check's spec follows the same form in its task.
- **The six hand lists.** `PAGES` and `EDITED` gain `ValidatePanel` (its edited field the calibration's "Test datasets"), `WALKED` its three shapes (the whole list; a check's page beside the list; a check's page in its place), `REBUILT` gains `validate_tab.py` alone, and so does the page tuple of `tests/test_nav_and_gating.py:4285` (both are equality pins over the files that hold a `StepPage` subclass); a second tuple, `CHECK_FILES = ("check_page.py", "check_results.py", "check_forms.py")`, joins the no-message-box loop (`tests/test_applied_and_pending.py:1118`); the string scan of `tests/test_nav_and_gating.py:4287` reads `check_page`, `check_results`, `check_forms`, `check_catalogue`, `check_readings` and `check_lineage` too, since they hold the words the check pages show; the step set `{"model", "prior", "training", "checks"}`; `PLACES["ValidatePanel"]` stays `("inference", "checks")`; `COUNTED_READINGS` holds `read_records` from Task 3. `test_the_pages_walked_here_are_every_page_built_on_the_step_page` is the net. The firm requirement's two tests gain the Checks list with a check's page beside it (1,400 wide) and in its place (900).

## Re-plan of 11 November 2026 (the owner's)

On 11 November 2026, during Task 5's fix round, the owner said they were unhappy with how long the redesign was taking
and how broad the program had grown: "I think I just want it to be a SBI parameter inference software, I don't think
the CMR of FDT stuff needs to be in it". Asked whether to take out the effective temperature (FDT) and the Reduction map
right after Task 5, they answered "Yes, along with the live simulation." Asked in their own words how else to speed the
work up ("Can we combine stages and tasks and be more efficient?") and offered six changes, they answered "Yes, all of
it."

This section overrides "How to run this plan" from Task 5b on: its execution order, its preparation of every building
task, its per-task loop, its departures rule and its hand-over rule. Everything else in this plan stands: the Global
Constraints (except that Task 5b opens the engine for itself alone, by the owner's yes), the Review Focus, the registry,
the real-screen run, the usage rule, the checks after each gate and every rule of "Every agent".

### The order

Tasks 0 to 5 done, then 5b (the cut), then four tasks built from the eight sections that are left (Task 6-7, Task 8-9,
Task 10-11, Task 12-13), then the last review, then Task 14 (the exit) as written. After this stage: the code audit
(its list, less what the cut removes), the retrain from the window, then ONE last stage for what the design's stages 6
to 9 held (Records, Your own models, Help and the first-time opening's help, the engine's sentences, the guide pages, the
owner's walkthrough rows, the final gates), planned then, with Help and the rewording trimmed to what is needed.

### The loop from Task 5b

- **One builder** (opus), fresh for each task, works in the repository from this plan's sections for the task, the items
  carried to it and the controller's dispatch. It reads the code, writes each test first and sees it red for its stated
  reason, builds, commits by logical step (each commit's suites green), and writes its report. Where a section leaves a
  choice a person would meet, or the builder finds the plan wrong, it writes a short note and stops for the controller's
  ruling before it builds that part. No skeptic's trial, no brief written out block by block, no separate implementer.
- **On DONE:** the one-process fast gate in the background and, at the same moment, two readers. One (sonnet) reads the
  sections against what was built: completeness, the counts, the words, the rule that nothing shippable cites the working
  record. One (opus) reads the product before against after on the real devices, drawn where it draws, and the tests for
  the faults they would miss; it runs faults only where a test looks unable to fail, and only after the gate has exited.
- **One fix round** through the builder, resumed, with the controller's rulings written in the ledger, then a re-read by
  one reader (sonnet) while a fresh gate runs on the final head. A task is complete when its review is clean and its gate
  is green on the same head. What the re-read leaves open is ruled, carried to a named task, or brought to the owner.
- **The last review** keeps its three readers, seat three's fault runs and one fix wave, over the whole stage.
- **Departures:** one row each in this plan's table, with its cost and who decided; not in the design's section 8. A
  ruling is a ledger line; there are no rulings files.
- **Hand-over:** at the end of a task when the usage rule says stop, or when the chat's room runs low; not by a count of
  tasks.

### Task 5b: SBI only (the owner's cut)

**Goes.**
- The effective-temperature measurement with its CrossVal study, its sweeps and its comparisons: `core/FDT/` whole;
  `core/Helpers/fdt.py` unless something kept uses it; `core/tool/fdt.py` and the subcommands `fdt`, `crossval` and
  `compare` (`cells`, `repeats`, `renormalise`, `sweeps`); the `fdt` record kind in `core/artifacts/` and wherever the
  tool, the window and the Records screen name, list, show or compare it; the Effective temperature place
  (`core/gui/panels/fdt_panel.py`, `crossval_panel.py`, `core/gui/screens/temperature_screen.py`,
  `core/gui/widgets/compare_list.py`, the parts of `core/gui/panels/record_view.py` that serve it); the FDT settings
  (`FDTConfig` in `core/sim_config.py`, the FDT constants in `core/config.py`, `cli.make_fdt_config`) and every entry in
  the refusals, the fields tables, the registry, the seeds and the help defaults that serves only them.
- The Reduction map: `core/Reduction/` whole with its tests, `core/gui/panels/reduction_panel.py`, its place.
- Live simulation: `core/gui/panels/simulate_panel.py`, `simulate_runner.py`, `simulate_export.py`, its place, and what
  only it uses (`core/gui/widgets/live_hair_bundle.py`, a video export, a figure helper), with `tests/test_simulate.py`.
- Their tests in the other suites (`tests/test_fdt_user.py`, `tests/test_fdt_compare.py`; the FDT, CrossVal and compare
  tests of `tests/test_tool.py`, two of them the slow set's `test_fdt_runs_the_nadrowski_sanity_checks_end_to_end` and
  `test_fdt_and_crossval_run_at_tiny_size`; the `fdt` kind's tests in `tests/test_artifact_store.py` and
  `tests/test_artifact_browser.py`; the removed places in `tests/test_shell.py`, `tests/test_nav_and_gating.py`,
  `tests/test_step_chain.py`, `tests/test_settings_persistence.py` and the rest) and the shared stand-ins that serve only
  them (`build_fdt_record`, `compare_preflight_refusals` in `tests/_fixtures.py`). A standing test that walks every place
  or every kind keeps walking what is left; it is not deleted.
- Their words in `docs/guide/` (pages and sentences), Help, Home, the first-time page, `README.md`, and the rows of
  `docs/checklists/display-walkthrough.md` that exercise a removed page (marked retired with the date, not deleted).

**Stays.** Everything the inference uses: `core/Simulator/`, `core/Solvers/`, `core/Models/`, `core/SBI/`,
`core/diagnostics/`, `core/forcing.py`, `cli.parse_cell` and all that Your own models uses; Records, less the `fdt`
kind. `Resources/` is not touched, and no package leaves `requirements.txt` (one that only removed code used is named in
the report). Comments in kept files that name removed code are rewritten to say the same in words, except inside a
function of `core.SBI.fit_state.FIT_CODE`, which stay as they are.

**What must not change, each held by a test or a check written in the report.**
1. The simulation set's identity: `SimulationIdentity`, `_canonical`, `identity_digest` and `resolve_dir` unchanged, and
   the finished set `Artifacts/simulations/eda5c2a1e468` still recognised: its manifest read (read only) and its identity
   recomputed equal, before and after.
2. No function of `core.SBI.fit_state.FIT_CODE` touched: `test_the_named_training_code_is_as_recorded` green.
3. `core/config.py`: only the FDT constants go; every other line byte for byte. `SimConfig` untouched.
4. Anything on disk under `Artifacts/`: nothing is deleted, moved or written there. A store root holding an `fdt/`
   folder (empty, as the owner's is, and one holding a record) lists, loads and cleans as before without touching it.
5. The settings file: keys a removed page wrote, in a scratch `PRISM.ini`, are ignored at launch, never restored and
   never deleted.
6. The command line: `python -m core --help` lists no removed subcommand, and `docs/guide/command-line.md` agrees
   (`tests/test_docs.py`).
7. The window: the rail, Home, Help, the keys and the first-time page name no removed place; every remaining place opens;
   the Records screen offers no `fdt` kind.
8. A standing scan: no file under `core/`, `tests/`, `docs/guide/`, nor `README.md`, `conftest.py`, `run.bat` or
   `run.sh`, imports or names a removed module or file; `tests/test_source_hygiene.py`'s allowlist loses the entries
   that only removed files needed.

**Commits** (each with its tests, each green): the window's pages; the command line, the engine and the store; the
guide, the walkthrough and `README.md`.

**Gates.** The fast gate on the final head (its new count stated), the slow set (three tests left), the display tests.
The card gate only if a file under `core/SBI/`, `core/Simulator/`, `core/Solvers/`, `core/Models/` or `core/diagnostics/`
changes beyond comments.

**After it, the controller** re-bases the exit's checks on Task 5b's head: `exit_fence.py`'s expected engine changes
become Task 2's plus the files Task 5b's report lists; `stage-5/gates/digests-at-start.json` is taken again there (the
seventeen digests of the named training code unchanged); `len(FIELDS)` becomes the count Task 5b's report states; and
Task 14's walkthrough rows that exercise a removed page are dropped from its list. Then it shortens `CLAUDE.md` to its
rules. The owner's yes of 11 November covers two edits: the builder's, of the sentences that describe the removed
parts, and the controller's shortening, with the substance of every rule kept; a change to what a rule says still
needs the owner's yes on its exact words. The card gate's block (the five command lines, run 5's drill and their pass
criteria) stays whole, in `CLAUDE.md` or moved whole into `docs/guide/testing.md` with a pointer, since STATE's gate
table and that page carry it verbatim and Task 14 runs it. The controller shows the owner the result and updates
`docs/STATE.md`.

### Tasks 6 to 13 as four

- **Task 6-7:** Task 6's and Task 7's sections (the forms and runs of every check), with the items carried to both.
- **Task 8-9:** Task 8's and Task 9's sections.
- **Task 10-11:** Task 10's and Task 11's sections.
- **Task 12-13:** Task 12's and Task 13's sections.

Each builder reads both sections. Where a section names a file, test, place, key or walkthrough row that Task 5b removed
(for example `tests/test_simulate.py`, `tests/test_fdt_user.py`, Live simulation's boxes, the five FDT keys in Task 7's
set with no control), that part falls away and any count it states is counted again.

**Carried into Task 6-7: labels shown as windows of their own** (found by Task 5's fix round on the real screen; older
code). Fourteen labels in each display test that builds Parameter inference are shown as windows of their own before
they have a parent: `core/gui/widgets/form.py`, `Row.__init__` (the unit label, `self.unit.setVisible(...)`) and
`core/gui/panels/inference/step_page.py`'s constructor (`self.plan_line.setVisible(...)`). They flash once a launch in
the real program. The cure: a parent first, then the visibility. Its test is a standing one, the cheap guard Task 5's
fix round measured (a wrapper over `setVisible` and `show` during the builders, no event filter): no widget the window's
builders make is shown as a window of its own, except the window itself and the dialogs the guards already record.

- **The rows (9.4).** Band: "Cell file" (`cell`, main; the cells of the model's folder in a keeping list, opening on step 5's cell or on nothing); folded "Recording lengths (s)" (`probe_lengths`, blank: None, five chosen by the engine; else numbers separated by commas or spaces), "Simulations per point" (`band_repeats`, 24), "Seed" (`probe_seed`, 0). Audit: "Batches to audit" (`mask_num_runs`, 100); folded "Simulations per batch" (`mask_run_size`, 32), "Seed" (`probe_seed`, 0). Calibration: "Test datasets" (`n_cal`, 2,000), "Operating points" (`cal_n_scales`, 200); folded "Samples per dataset" (`num_posterior_samples`, 1,000), "Seed" (`seed`, blank: one drawn by `random.randrange(2**31)` at the press, passed, and named in the result). Input use: folded "Simulations read" (`rows`, 200,000), "Points per sweep" (`n_sweep`, 33). Identifiability: folded "Weakest directions totalled" (`n_worst`, 3), "Largest contributions shown" (`top_n`, 4). Whole-number boxes `IntField(grouped=True)`, right-aligned, the 120 px width; nothing on a form is remembered between launches (U15); the calibration's three rows are built in the constructor (a plan fills them in Task 12).

Calibration, input use and identifiability take `session.cfg`, `session.posterior`, `session.inf_prior` as today's calibration does. Each call sends `name`, `note` (5.5: `NameAndNote("calibration" | "diagnostic", required=False)`), `provide_fig_sink=True`, the store left to the process default.

## Task 8: The timing of a short fit and the card test

**Implements:** 9.6, 9.7, E3's screen (11.4), B25, C28, 6.3.3's fall-back to the timing check, 6.5's `check` entry for the two checks that write no record; departures 405, 411, 415, 423 (its reason goes); departure 123's last field; W092, W188's other half. Carried-over rows: `tests/test_run_states.py:1408-1420` (`_DISPATCHES` gains `check_scratch.short_fit`, `card_check`, each dispatched by its name), `:1835-1844` (two rows); `tests/test_nav_and_gating.py:1444` (the pin of `CONTROL["num_runs"]`) and the four sentences of `:1353-1440` that the new place changes.

**Files:**
- Create: `core/gui/check_scratch.py`
- Modify: `core/gui/panels/inference/check_forms.py`, `core/gui/panels/inference/validate_tab.py`, `core/gui/panels/inference/check_results.py`, `core/gui/check_readings.py`, `core/gui/panels/inference/posterior_tab.py` (`expected()`), `core/gui/fields.py` (`num_runs`, `run_size_cap`, `hidden_features`, `num_transforms` gain the place "checks"), `core/gui/run/model.py` (`facts["keeps_nothing"]`), `core/gui/run/controller.py` (`check_result`), `core/gui/run/history.py` (`estimate`), `tests/test_checks.py`, `tests/test_run_states.py`, `tests/test_run_text.py`, `tests/test_nav_and_gating.py`

**Interfaces:** Produces `check_scratch.SCRATCH_PREFIX = "prism_check_"`, `RMTREE_TRIES = 5`, `RMTREE_PAUSE_S = 0.2`; `scratch_store(*, prefix: str = SCRATCH_PREFIX, pause=time.sleep) -> ContextManager[ArtifactStore]`; `@dataclass(frozen=True) ShortFit(check_result: dict)`, `short_fit(cfg, prior, *, num_runs: int, run_size_cap: int, epochs: int, hidden_features: int, num_transforms: int, fig_sink=None, store_factory=scratch_store) -> ShortFit`; `@dataclass(frozen=True) CardCheck(check_result: dict)`, `card_check(cfg, *, cell: str, t_obs_s: float, seed: int, num_runs: int, run_size_cap: int, n_cal: int, epochs: int, hidden_features: int, num_transforms: int, on_stage, fig_sink=None, store_factory=scratch_store) -> CardCheck`; `CARD_STAGE_WORDS = {"prior": "Prior", "posterior": "Training", "validate": "Calibration", "infer": "Inference"}`; `PosteriorPanel.expected() -> tuple[int, str] | None` (the expected epochs its last reading resolved). Consumes `orchestrator.build_posterior`, `core.diagnostics.card_test`, `ArtifactStore`, `readings.read`, `RunView.set_stage`.

**Named training code:** none edited; `build_posterior` and `card_test` called. Neither function is changed, so a fit that is never interrupted computes what it computes today.

Rules the brief must hold:
- **The scratch store (9.6).** `scratch_store()` makes `tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=SCRATCH_PREFIX)`, yields `ArtifactStore(folder)` passed as `store=`, and removes the folder in `finally` however the block ends (finished, failed, stopped by `WorkerCancelled`): references to the loaded records dropped first, `shutil.rmtree` tried `RMTREE_TRIES` times `RMTREE_PAUSE_S` apart (Windows may hold `posterior.pt` open), a folder that still cannot go logged once as a warning naming it. `use_store` is never called. Nothing is written in the default store.
- **The short fit (9.6, departure 411).** `short_fit` calls `build_posterior(cfg, prior, None, True, store=<scratch>, num_runs=, run_size_cap=, hidden_features=, num_transforms=, max_num_epochs=epochs - 1, checkpoint_every=0, resume="auto", fig_sink=)`, the prior in use handed as the object. For the call it attaches a recording `logging.Handler` to the `core` logger (removed in `finally`; it only notes): the time (`record.created`) of the first record that ends generation (`[patho] run total` or `[chi] masked probes`, read by `readings.read`) and the "took" seconds of each `[fit] epoch` record. `check_result = {"network": [h, t], "simulations": <num_runs × the batch's rows>, "epochs": <training.epochs_trained>, "span": <manifest.created − generation's end, in seconds>, "per_epoch": span / epochs, "median_epoch": <median of the took seconds>, "press": <the press's id>}`, the press in the result alone, never in `sizes`, so that `history.last_check` finds the time at the same sizes; each None when its source is missing; never a division by nought. It returns no `kind`, `id` or `name`.
- **Two runs, one press (departure 411).** "Run this check" dispatches `self.dispatch(check_scratch.short_fit, …)` for the first network, the Training form's width and depth (`posterior_panel.form.rows["hidden_features"]`, `["num_transforms"]`); at that run's `run_ended` with `outcome == "finished"` the page asks for the second one turn later (`QTimer.singleShot(0, …)`, as `LoadChain._ended` starts its next link, since `_end` still draws the first run's end after `run_ended`), and dispatches the built-in `NSF_HIDDEN_FEATURES` × `NSF_NUM_TRANSFORMS` (`config.py:364-365`), the same `press`, only if no run is live then; when the two are equal, one run. A stop, a failure or a refusal of the first starts no second. Each run's spec: `RunSpec("Check", ("inference", "checks"), name="Timing of a short fit", stop_button="Stop this check…", stages=(Stage("Rotation", …), Stage("Simulation", …), Stage("Fit", …)) begun at a training's rows (`base.TRAINING_STAGES`' begins), kinds=(), records=(), simulation_set=False, retries=False, facts={…, "keeps_nothing": True}, plan=screen.plan_entry(), check={"key": "timing", "sizes": {"num_runs", "run_size_cap", "epochs", "network"}})`. `facts["keeps_nothing"]` keeps the run's page from raising the "not kept" message (`readings` rows `fit_not_kept`, `fit_saving_off`).
- **The timing's result (9.6).** From the two newest entries of one `check_result["press"]`: "Timing of a short fit: done. One epoch of the 256 × 10 network took 7.4 s on 40,960 simulations; of the 128 × 8 network, 5.2 s." Table "Network", "Epochs timed", "Span", "Seconds per epoch", "Median epoch". Then the projection by the runbook's rule, seconds per epoch × (planned simulations ÷ timed simulations) × E ÷ 3,600, planned from the Training form's batches and simulations per batch, E from `posterior_panel.expected()`: "About 134 hours of fitting, if the fit takes 130 epochs (the last training of this kind, retrain_aug)."; with no E: "There is no epoch count to project with. Type one in "Expected epochs" in step 3." No stop point, no pass.
- **Its form.** "Batches" (`num_runs`, 20), "Epochs" (row key `epochs`, label "Epochs", 10, at least 2: "At least 2 epochs: the limit sent is one less."); folded, read-only: "Networks: 256 by 10, as step 3's form holds them, then the built-in 128 by 8." "Before you start": "Trains two short fits, one per network, each on 20 batches for 10 epochs; each computes its own rotation first." (one fit when equal: "Trains one short fit …"), the time from the history, the memory lines, "Nothing else can run until it ends.", "Nothing is kept but the result."
- **The card test (9.7, departure 405).** `card_check` calls `core.diagnostics.card_test(cfg, cell=, t_obs_s=, seed=, num_runs=, run_size_cap=, n_cal=, max_num_epochs=epochs - 1, hidden_features=, num_transforms=, checkpoint=False, save=False, on_stage=<the page's>, fig_sink=, store=<scratch>)`, with `cfg` built truth-free from the draft and the bounds file (`make_config` with no cell loaded, as `smoke` builds it), and the same recording handler, which notes the first `[chi] masked probes` record's counts and how many batches had to be split (the `oom_split_*` rows). `check_result = {"seconds": dict(result.seconds), "stages_run": list(result.stages_run), "unusable": <share or None>, "split": <count>}`; the `CardTestResult` and its five records are dropped before the folder goes. The page dispatches it by its name (`self.dispatch(check_scratch.card_check, …)`). `on_stage` only emits a queued signal (`_CardStages(QObject)`, private, so the builders' scan does not name it; `stage = Signal(str, str, object)`) and never raises; the page's slot calls `run_view.set_stage(CARD_STAGE_WORDS[stage])` and keeps each call, since a failure returns no result. Spec: `RunSpec("Check", ("inference", "checks"), name="Card test", stop_button="Stop this check…", stages=tuple(Stage(w) for w in CARD_STAGE_WORDS.values()), kinds=(), records=(), simulation_set=False, retries=False, facts={…, "keeps_nothing": True}, plan=screen.plan_entry(), check={"key": "card_test", "sizes": {"num_runs", "run_size_cap", "n_cal", "epochs", "network", "t_obs"}})`.
- **Its form (9.4).** "Cell file" (`cell`), "Recording length" (`t_obs`, 1.0); folded "Batches" (`num_runs`, 4), "Simulations per batch" (`run_size_cap`, 32), "Test datasets" (`n_cal`, 40), "Epochs" (6), "Network width" and "Network depth" (as step 3's form holds them), "Seed" (`seed`, 0, Task 7's rule). "Before you start": "This builds a prior at full size and then trains a small posterior, calibrates it and runs one inference on a simulated cell: four real stages, the last three at very small sizes, on the graphics card. It takes minutes (5 min 36 s here last time). Nothing else can run meanwhile, and nothing it makes is kept." (with no run on this computer: "… It takes minutes; it has not run on this computer yet. …"); the memory lines.
- **The card test's result.** "Card test: done. Every stage ran on the graphics card in 5 min 36 s." or "Card test: failed in Training." (the history's `stage` at the failure); table "Stage", "Seconds", "Last time"; "Unusable driven recordings in its training: 41.2 %. At this size a share within about 12 points of 37 % says nothing either way."; "Batches split for lack of memory: 0."; "This shows that the chain runs. It says nothing about calibration: at these sizes the test has no power." Its calibration's verdict is not shown. Its row reads "Done" only from a finished entry.
- **The history (6.5, 6.3.3).** `controller._on_result` copies a payload's `check_result` (a dict of names and numbers) into the run's `check["result"]`; a payload with no `kind` notes no record. `history.estimate(..., per="epoch")` with no same-word run of that network and those simulations takes the newest timing entry of the same network: pace `per_epoch × size["simulations"] ÷ result["simulations"]`, source "the timing of a short fit on 3 Oct 2026, 14:02, scaled to these simulations".
- **Controls.** `num_runs` and `run_size_cap` read `(("training", "checks", "narrowing"), …)`, `hidden_features` and `num_transforms` `(("training", "checks"), …)`, in the window's order; their `fix_sentence` with no page named then names the three or two steps. `NOT_BUILT_TEXT` goes with its last user.

**Files:**
- Modify: `core/gui/run/model.py`, `core/gui/run/controller.py`, `core/gui/run/history.py`, `core/gui/run/view.py`, `core/gui/run/questions.py`, `core/gui/panels/inference/base.py` (`training_spec(..., audit=None, prior=None)`), `core/gui/panels/inference/posterior_tab.py`, `core/gui/panels/inference/tsnpe_tab.py` (hands the audit and the prior), `core/gui/panels/inference/validate_tab.py` (check 3's acceptance), `core/gui/check_lineage.py` (the acceptance read from the history), `tests/_fixtures.py` (`COUNTED_READINGS`: `runs_for_set_id`), `tests/test_run_states.py`, `tests/test_run_text.py`, `tests/test_checks.py`, `tests/test_stop_and_close.py`

**Interfaces:** Produces R-health's signatures; `RunModel.masked() -> Masked | None`; `RunModel.comparison() -> Comparison | None`; `RunController.answer_comparison(answer: str) -> None` (`"accepted"` or `"keep_training"`); `RunController.amend(run: str, change: Callable[[dict], None]) -> dict | None`; `history.runs_for_set_id(folder: Path, set_id: str) -> list[dict]`; `training_spec(word, place, stop_button, cfg, kwargs, *, narrowed, expected_epochs=None, plan=None, settings=None, audit: dict | None = None, prior: str | None = None) -> RunSpec`. Consumes `check_readings.compare`, `Comparison`, `checks_held`.

**Named training code:** none.

Rules the brief must hold:
- **The first record (6.3.5).** The model keeps the FIRST `chi_total` or `chi_none` reading of a run whose word is in `questions.TRAINING_WORDS` ("Training", "Narrowing round") as `Masked(unusable: int, total: int, batches: int, scope: str)` (`chi_none`: `total` 0, `batches` 0); later ones change nothing; a run of another word keeps none and draws no "Training health" table (a calibration's and a card test's lines are not a training's).
- **What the run is handed.** At the press, the Training page (and the round's, Task 11; the old round tab until then) hands `training_spec(..., audit=, prior=)`, which puts into `RunSpec.facts` `"prior"` the prior's id and `"audit"` the audit that counts for it from `checks_held` (`{"id", "fraction", "batches"}`, names and numbers) or None. The comparison is made against that audit; the run reads nothing while live (departure 418: one run at a time, so no audit can be written meanwhile, and Records changes nothing while a run is live).
- **The comparison,** computed once at the first record by `compare(unusable=, total=, scope=, audit_fraction=, accepted=None, batches=, prior_words=, kept_set=<spec.simulation_set and cadence above 0>)`. The "Unusable driven recordings" row reads its state: "Fine" for "Passed", else the check's word ("Look closer", "Failed", "Waiting for the audit", "Not compared"); its sentence the comparison's. One "Attention" message for "Look closer", "Waiting for the audit" and "Not compared", in the comparison's words (the counts are in the sentence, so the message carries no count of its own). Above 5 points: the row "Failed", an "Attention" message with the advice of Task 3's `compare`, and under the health table two buttons, "Stop training…" (the run's own stop question, U12) and "Keep training"; `RunStatus.needs_you` reads "The comparison with the audit needs an answer." until one is pressed; the rail's foot says "<word> needs you", Home its next line, the taskbar flashes once through `this_computer`. The window never stops the fit: no comparison state calls `stop`.
- **The answers.** `answer_comparison("keep_training")` (from the run's page) and `answer_comparison("accepted")` (from check 3's page while the training is live) hold the answer on the `_Run`, clear `needs_you`, and `_record` writes `health["comparison"]` with every key of `COMPARISON_FIELDS = ("unusable", "total", "scope", "batches", "audit", "audit_fraction", "difference", "state", "accepted", "kept_training")`, `accepted` = `{"at": <iso>, "audit": <id>, "difference": D}`, `kept_training` = `<iso>`, each None until given.
- **After the run (departure 410).** `RunController.amend(run, change)` is the one public call: for the run the controller still holds (its end not yet seen, or seen and not discarded), it applies `change` to the record it holds and writes it as `_write_ended` does, so the next write of that run's end (`acknowledge` through `_write_seen`, `discard` through `_gone`) keeps the change; for any other run it calls `history.amend(folder: Path, run: str, change: Callable[[dict], None]) -> dict | None`, the file half: reads the run's file, applies `change`, writes the whole record by replace as `history.write` writes it; None for a file gone or unreadable; never raises. `RunController._rewrite` calls `history.amend` too. `history.runs_for` keeps its whole-path match for its one caller, `RunController._name_the_set` (`controller.py:1403`), which passes the folder as the run names it. Check 3 reads a training's history through a second reader, `history.runs_for_set_id(folder, set_id)`: the runs whose `simulation_set["folder"]`'s last part (`Path(...).name`, whatever its slashes) is `set_id`, oldest first; a counted reading, made in `read_checks` only.
- **Check 3's page.** "Look closer" shows 9.4's table (simulation set; audit: share, driven recordings counted, batches, per-batch median and range) and the plain button "Accept this difference": during the training, `answer_comparison("accepted")`; after it, `controller().amend(<the newest training run of runs_for_set_id(folder, S) that holds health.comparison>, change)`. From then check 3 reads "Passed" with "A difference of 3.8 points was accepted on 3 Oct 2026, 09:14."; the acceptance is of the audit whose id it holds and lapses when another audit counts. For a set no history names, the button is greyed with "This simulation set was made outside the window: there is no run history to keep the acceptance in." A "Keep training" answer shows there: "You chose to keep training on 2 Oct 2026, 09:14." An answer never changes the state, and it is kept once: a run reopened after PRISM is closed (its past view, Home) reads the answer from its history and asks nothing again.

Recordings: "Spontaneous recording" (`recording_spont`, a file row), then by the posterior's kind: forced, "Driven recording" (`recording_forced`) and the drive's own rows (`drive_amplitude`, `drive_frequency`, `drive_phase`, as `fields._drive` labels them, blank), made once per forcing name seen and hidden; frequency response, "Driven recordings" (`recording_probe`, a `RowTable` of rows "file at frequency Hz", "Add a driven recording", "Plan the driven recordings…" under it) and "Drive amplitude" (`chi_f0_si`, unit N, blank); "Recording length" in both. Folded in both: "Posterior draws" (`n_samples`, `IntField(grouped=True)`, usual the default of `inspect.signature(orchestrator.infer_and_visualize).parameters["n_samples"]`, 1,000). Names: `NameAndNote("inference", required=False, label="Name of the inference", other=OtherName("observation_name", "observation", "Name of the observation", True))`. Under the length: "The posterior was trained on lengths from 1 to 60 s." from the posterior's `manifest.config["t_min_exp"]`, `["t_max_exp"]` (cell units: the configuration's time unit), else `config.T_MIN_EXP_S`, `T_MAX_EXP_S`. Origin words only beside "Posterior draws".
- **The driven recordings (8.5).** A row is one widget holding a file and a frequency. With no remembered rows the table opens with one blank row; remembered rows are all shown, none dropped. More rows than the posterior's slots (`posterior.manifest.body["conditioning"]["chi_k_pad"]`): `Fact("err", "This posterior takes at most 12 driven recordings; remove 2.", "too many driven recordings")`; "Add a driven recording" stays live. A configuration taken later redraws the fact, never the rows. Planning (at the press, on the window's thread, as today): the cell's own frequency measured from the spontaneous recording and the typed length; one sentence on the page, "The cell's own frequency is 12.3 Hz; the band runs from 0.37 to 3.7 Hz."; only blank frequencies filled, never a typed zero; beside each row (on the line under it) `driven_reading(chi.probe_verdict(...))`: used ("fine", "Fine, 23 drive cycles"); cut ("fine", "Fine, 20 drive cycles: only the first 3.2 s are used"); too short ("warn", "Unusable: 1.4 drive cycles, fewer than the 2 needed. Record at least 0.4 s at this frequency."); refused ("err", the verdict's reason as the engine words it, E11's to reword). A blank length at the press says "Type a recording length to plan the driven recordings." under the button. Removing a row: Task 4's question with `INFER_REMOVE` (departure 403).
- **The facts,** `inference_facts(*, mode, uses, narrowed_words, cell_problem, length, trained, checked, slots, rows, blanks, others_pending, name_facts, field_facts, plan_lines) -> tuple[Fact, ...]`, in 8.5's order: "Posterior retrain_posterior: frequency response, up to 12 driven recordings." (info); "Attention. This posterior was narrowed around the observation cell_a and is valid only near it."; the cell's problem ("Error. The cell gives no value for T.", blocking "the cell does not fit"); the length outside the trained range ("Attention. 75 s is above the 60 s the posterior was trained on; it may extrapolate." / "… below the 1 s …"); "Information. The cell is not checked against what training saw: the prior in use is not the one this posterior was trained with."; the slot fact; "Needs a posterior: train one in step 3, or load a saved one." (lock); the blank rows' facts ("Type a recording length.", "Type the drive amplitude.", "Choose the spontaneous recording." each `err` with its blocks words).
- **`read_disk()`:** the cell list repointed to `CELL_PATH / session.draft.model.lower()` and the remembered cell selected only when remembered for that model; `cli.validate_gt_file` of the cell picked against the configuration in use; the narrowed posterior's observation label (`store.get("observation", parents["observation"])`, or `NO_OBSERVATION_WORDS`). `COUNTED_READINGS` gains `cli.validate_gt_file` (`"cli": ("validate_gt_file",)`). The page takes the configuration as Training does, from `session.cfg` at drawing; a change of what is in use marks it stale and it reads at its next showing; `_fan_out` loses `infer_panel.on_config_built`.
- **The start.** "Run inference". A narrowed posterior (`posterior.posterior.x_obs_digest is not None`, the engine's predicate) asks first at every run in both modes: title "Run on another observation?", text "This posterior is valid only near the observation it was narrowed around, cell_a." then "A cell simulated now has new noise, so it is never that observation." or departure 413's "If these are the same recordings with the same description, PRISM finds them to be that observation and the inference runs as on it; your answer is then not recorded, though the observation is saved again as a new record." then "Elsewhere the result is an extrapolation. The inference records that you accepted this."; "Cancel" preselected, "Run inference anyway" passes `Accept(other_observation=True)`; never remembered. Then `dispatch(orchestrator.simulated_inference, cfg, posterior, t_obs, cell=<path> or gt_values=<grid>, prior=<the session's prior only when its id is the posterior's parents["prior"], else None>, accept=, n_samples=, name=, note=, observation_name=, if_name_taken="unnamed", provide_fig_sink=True, on_result=self._on_inference)` or `experimental_inference(cfg, posterior, RecordingSet(spont=, forced=, F0_si=, forcing_params_si=, T_obs_s=), accept=, n_samples=, name=, note=, observation_name=, if_name_taken="unnamed", provide_fig_sink=True, on_result=self._on_inference)` (no prior: the engine's recordings path takes none; 8.5's "each with `prior=`" holds for the simulated cell only). Spec: `RunSpec("Inference", ("inference", "infer"), name=<the inference's name>, stop_button="Stop the inference…", kinds=("observation", "inference"), settings=self.settings_entry())`, its stop and end sentences (row 99): "The inference finished." / "The inference was stopped; nothing was kept." After `dispatch` answers True, `put_many` of `inference_infer/infer_mode`, and in the simulated mode `cell` with `cell_model`, in the recordings mode the spontaneous path under the key of the kind in use (`exp_spont` or `chi_spont`), `exp_forced`, and `driven/size`, `driven/<n>/file`, `driven/<n>/frequency`. Closing writes nothing; the stale keys `sim_tobs`, `exp_tobs`, `chi_tobs`, `chi_f0_si` are ignored.
- **Afterwards (8.5).** `_on_inference((observation, inference))` calls `screen.take_observation(observation)` (writes `session.observation`, `refresh_gates()`, once, on the window's thread), then the result region: the table "Parameter", "5 %", "50 %", "95 %", "Unit" (+ "True value" for a simulated cell) from `results["posterior_summary"]` in the record's order, an assumed input's name followed by "(assumed input)", units from `parameter_units`; the pictures, "Posterior corner" first, then the record's others captioned by their titles, then the observation's trace; "Of the observation's features, 93 % fall inside what the posterior predicts (90 % interval)." left out when `coverage_90` is None; "Saved as the inference retrain_inf and the observation cell_a." (the record labels when unnamed). E7 for the observation: "It was saved without its name: 'cell_a' was taken while the run was going." The inference's own name refused after its observation was saved (taken meanwhile): the observation's name is emptied and the page says "The observation was saved as cell_a; give the inference another name." Next steps: `NextStep("Narrow around this observation", "narrowing", {"observation": observation.id})` and `NextStep("Run another inference", "infer")` (empties the result, the names and the note; stays on the page). Until Task 11 the old round page takes the carried observation by today's means: `InferenceScreen.open_with` finds no `take_carried` on it, so the screen selects the id in `tsnpe_panel.obs_picker` itself (its `restore_key`); Task 11 replaces this with the rebuilt page's `take_carried`.

**Files:**
- Modify: `core/gui/panels/inference/tsnpe_tab.py` (whole), `core/gui/panels/inference/posterior_tab.py` (`_RULES` and `_row_problem` move out), `core/gui/panels/inference/base.py` (`_TrainingBudgetMixin`, `_StagePanel`, the docstrings go), `core/gui/panels/inference_tabs.py`, `core/gui/step_facts.py`, `core/gui/fields.py`, `core/gui/panels/inference/help_text.py`, `core/gui/remembered.py`, `core/gui/run/controller.py` (`_expected`'s words), `core/gui/screens/inference_screen.py` (the carried observation now through `take_carried`), the suites above, `tests/_fixtures.py` (`COUNTED_READINGS`: `observation_digests`), `tests/_runs.py`

**Interfaces:** Produces `TSNPEPanel(StepPage)` (`step="narrowing"`), `TSNPEPanel.observation_on_offer() -> bool` (from its held reading), `TSNPEPanel.take_carried(carries)` (selects `carries["observation"]` in its list), `TSNPEPanel.settings_entry() -> dict`, `tsnpe_tab.observation_digests(store) -> dict[str, str]` (each observation's id and digest, its manifest read once by folder; a counted reading), `step_facts.FIT_RULES`, `step_facts.fit_row_problem(key: str, value) -> str`, `step_facts.round_facts(...)`, `step_facts.ROUND_SIMULATIONS_TEXT`. Consumes `orchestrator.tsnpe_round`, `store.load_observation`, `training_preview`'s width and runs, `history.estimate`, `controller().expected_epochs`, `limit_words`, `pre_run_fact`, `pre_run_question`, `take_posterior`.

**Named training code:** none edited; `tsnpe_round` called. The network rows are passed as arguments (`hidden_features`, `num_transforms`); `build_embedding_net` and `expected_forcing_dim` are not touched.

Rules the brief must hold:
- **The rows,** 8.6's table: "Observation" (`observation`, a keeping `StorePicker("observation", keeps_selection=True)` listing the observations of the configuration's kind and width; its built-in value the observation the session holds, else the remembered one; carried by "Narrow around this observation"); "Region level" (`hpd_level`, 0.999, `truncate.DEFAULT_HPD`); "Directions narrowed" (`n_directions`, 5, `truncate.DEFAULT_N_DIRECTIONS`); "Batches" (`num_runs`, 5,000, remembered apart from Training's); "Simulations per batch" (`run_size_cap`, `TickedField(IntField(grouped=True), "Automatic", 0)`, remembered); "Network width", "Network depth" (`hidden_features`, `num_transforms`, 128 and 8, or the plan's); folded "Learning rate", "End the fit after … epochs without improvement", "Epoch limit" (`TickedField`, "No limit", None), "Save the simulations every … batches" (`TickedField`, "Never", 0); then a text row "Rotation: the posterior's own is reused."; "Name" (required) and "Note" (`NameAndNote("posterior", required=True)`). The rules of the eight shared rows are `step_facts.FIT_RULES` and `fit_row_problem`, which the Training page now reads too (moved, not copied). Under the network rows: "The posterior in use was trained at 256 by 10." from `posterior.manifest.body["training"]`, as a "warn" line when the two differ: "The posterior in use was trained at 256 by 10; this round trains 128 by 8.". "Region level" below `REGION_WARN_BELOW = 0.99` (the engine's `DEFAULT_HPD - 0.009`, pinned by a test): "Attention. Tighter than 0.99. Narrowing removes part of the prior for good; no later round gets it back." under it. "Directions narrowed" above the parameters' count blocks as typed: "At most 13: the model has 13 parameters." No "Continue or start new", no "Expected epochs".
- **The observation (W009, departure 409).** A new observation in the session (`take_observation`) moves the list to it and, when that replaces a pick, the line under it says "Changed to the observation just made, cell_b. Before: cell_a."; never while a run is live (at `run_ended`). With a narrowed posterior in use, the observations whose digest (`observation_digests(store)`, read in `read_disk`, never in `refresh_local_gates`) is not the posterior's are listed greyed with "Cannot be used: this posterior was narrowed around another observation" (departure 421). `observation_on_offer()` is True when the list holds one that can be chosen; the screen's `recompute_states` reads it (departure 72 closes). "Narrow around this observation" (Task 10) now reaches the rebuilt page's `take_carried`, which selects the carried id in the list; the screen's interim selection of Task 10 goes. A new posterior in use keeps the observation in use (the observation is the posterior's data, not its product: a round's posterior was made around it); a new prior, a new configuration, lets it go, as `reset_downstream` does today.
- **"Before you start",** `round_facts(*, uses, prior_own, simulations, continuing_from, result, checks, fit, how_long, memory, others_pending, name_facts, field_facts, plan_lines) -> tuple[Fact, ...]` in 8.6's order: "Posterior retrain_posterior and its prior retrain_prior; observation cell_b, a simulated cell, 4.5 s." (info); "Fine." or `Fact("err", "This posterior was trained with retrain_prior; the prior in use is other_prior. Load retrain_prior in step 2.", "the prior is not the posterior's own")`; C11's `ROUND_SIMULATIONS_TEXT`: "Simulates 10,240,000 simulations in the round's own simulation set. A round never continues a training's simulations; if this same round was run before with the same settings, its saved simulations are continued." (the count from `training_preview`'s runs and width), or with Continue "Continues this round's simulation set from batch 236."; `Fact("warn", "The posterior made here replaces the one in use in steps 3 to 5 and is valid only near this observation.")`; Task 3's pre-run line; the fit lines in the fit-saving design's words (`FIT_SAVED_TEXT`, `READ_BACK_TEXT`; with "Never" `FIT_LOST_TEXT`); the time (departure 406): "Fit: about 40 epochs of about 2 minutes: the last narrowing round of this kind, retrain_round1, ran 40." from `history.estimate(word="Narrowing round", …)` and `controller().expected_epochs`, both read in `read_disk` and held, or "There is no earlier narrowing round to estimate the fit's length from."; the memory line; others pending (steps 1, 2, 3, 5); the names; the plan. (`body.training.tsnpe_acceptance`; shown for a resumed round as for any, probe 4) and "The posterior it came from, retrain_posterior, stays in Records; load it in step 3 to go back."; next steps `NextStep("Check this posterior", "checks")` and `NextStep("Run inference on the same observation", "infer")`.

**Interfaces:** Produces R-plans (the registry's signatures). Consumes `core.plans.ALLOWED` ("Calibration": `n_cal`, `cal_n_scales`, `num_posterior_samples`; "Inference": `n_samples`; "Narrowing round": `n_directions`, `hpd_level` and the eight fit settings), each page's `plan_fields()` and `plan_read(applied)`.

**Named training code:** none.

Rules the brief must hold:
- **`HEADING_OF`** keyed by `keys.STEPS`: `{"model": "Model and options", "prior": "Prior", "training": "Training", "checks": "Calibration", "infer": "Inference", "narrowing": "Narrowing round"}`; `FILLED = tuple(HEADING_OF.values())`; `source_of` keeps every heading's values.
- **What each step's run reads,** `READS: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]]`: model `("Model and options",)`; prior `("Model and options", "Prior")`; training `("Model and options", "Prior", "Training")`; checks `("Model and options", "Prior", "Calibration")`; infer `("Model and options", "Prior", "Inference")`; narrowing `("Model and options", "Prior", "Narrowing round")`. `headings_to(step) -> tuple[str, ...]` is `READS.get(step, ())`: a later step's run reads what the earlier steps hold, never Training's form.
- **The pages.** `ValidatePanel.plan_fields()` is the calibration's three rows (built in its constructor, Task 6), whichever check is open; `plan_read(applied)` those three values, `{}` when `applied`; `InferPanel`'s `{"n_samples": row}`; `TSNPEPanel`'s ten rows; each shows the origin words beside each row a plan fills, and the plan line under its heading (`plan_line` visible on every step of `HEADING_OF`). `InferenceScreen.step_pages()` lists six: `config_panel`, `prior_panel`, `posterior_panel`, `validate_panel`, `infer_panel`, `tsnpe_panel`.
- **`config_tab.py`.** The two lookups of `plans.HEADING_OF[page.step]` (about `:1093`, `:1239`) take every step; "Show what it holds" has a row with its usual value for every setting, `NOT_APPLIED_YET` goes; "Save as a plan…" writes the later headings' departures as it writes the first three's.
- **Facts from values (the code audit's item 21).** `plans.departures_from(source: PlanSource, read: Mapping[tuple[str, str], object], usual: Mapping[tuple[str, str], object], labels: Mapping[tuple[str, str], str]) -> tuple[str, ...]`, pure; `plan_facts(screen, headings)` builds its three mappings from the pages' `plan_read` and rows once, then calls it. A plan chosen and not filled reads "Not filled yet. In use: no plan." on every step.
- **The question before a fill (7.1.7)** lists the later steps' values it would replace, as the first three's; "Return to the usual values" resets every step.

- Modify: `core/gui/continuing.py`, `core/gui/chain.py`, `core/gui/load_chain.py`, `core/gui/remembered.py`, `core/gui/screens/inference_screen.py`, `core/gui/screens/home_screen.py`, `core/gui/main_window.py`, `core/gui/panels/inference/tsnpe_tab.py`, `core/gui/panels/inference/posterior_tab.py`, `core/gui/panels/inference/resume.py`, `core/gui/run/retry.py`, `core/gui/step_facts.py`, `tests/test_retry.py`, `tests/test_shell.py`, `tests/test_step_chain.py`, `tests/test_applied_and_pending.py`, `tests/test_run_states.py`, `tests/test_settings_persistence.py`, `tests/test_gpu_paths.py`, `tests/_fixtures.py`, `docs/superpowers/notes/2026-10-29-the-code-audits-list.md`

**Interfaces:** Produces R-round's Resume and Continue (the registry's signatures), `TSNPEPanel.show_kept(kept_id: str) -> None` (the kept round's row in sight, the keyboard ring on its Resume) and `step_facts.new_round_question(sentence: str) -> tuple[str, str, str, str]` (title, text, the safe answer "Cancel", the other). Consumes `fit_state.kept_fits`, `KeptFit` (`region`, `parents`, `config`, `posterior_name`, `can_resume`, `why_not`), `resume_problems`, `resume_draft`, `resume_config`, `resume.read_offers`, `history.read`, `fill_forms` (which knows the round from Task 12), `LoadChain`, `store.delete("fit", id)`, `orchestrator.tsnpe_round`, `ask_text`, Task 11's page.

**Named training code:** none edited; `tsnpe_round` called with `resume_fit`.

Rules the brief must hold:
- **The observation's link (departure 401).** `load_chain(model, options, bounds_file, prior, posterior=None, observation=None, *, fill=None, candidate=None, press="", where="")` no longer raises on `observation`: after the posterior's link has returned its record, `TSNPEPanel.load_observation(ident: str) -> bool` dispatches `ArtifactStore.load_observation` as a loads-only run (`RunSpec("Observation load", ("inference", "narrowing"), loads_only=True, keeps_files=False)`, "Loading…" at the rail's foot, no history, no text file), its `on_result` `screen.take_observation(obs)`. A link refused or failed ends the chain; the earlier links stay (B3); the sentence under the heading names the step it stopped at (`_record_named` gains the observation). `remembered.Picks` gains `observation: str | None` and `observation_words: str`, written with `inference_tsnpe/observation` when an observation comes into use; Home's "Continue with these" passes it and its sentence names it: "…, the posterior retrain_round1 and the observation cell_b".
- **The offers (`continuing`).** `Offer.step: str = "training"` ("narrowing" for a round). A kept row with a region: kind "round", resumable as a training's "resume" is (`why_not` from `KeptFit`), its sentence `resume_sentence` in a round's words: "It stopped unexpectedly after epoch 41, last saved Sat 3 Oct, 03:12. Best validation loss -3.10, 2 epochs ago. It trains around the observation cell_b, narrowed from the posterior retrain_posterior, on the simulation set 7c1e09aa2f31 and the prior retrain_prior. Resuming first reads the simulation set back, about four minutes at full size."; no command. `offers` reads the histories of both words, "Training" and "Narrowing round", each word's newest alone, by the same rule (`_history_offer`); a round's history offers "continue" or "fit_again" with `step="narrowing"` only when its `settings` name `parent_posterior` and `observation` (Task 11's `settings_entry()` writes both), so a round run before this stage is offered no Continue (Home says how it ended). `for_the_steps` stays Training's (`step == "training"`); `for_the_round(found) -> Offer | None` the newest round's "round" or "continue" that can be taken. `ROUND_BUTTON`, `ROUND_TEXT`, `round_command` go.
- **The chain and Home (S5-2, departure 399).** `step_states(..., kept_round=None)`: step 6, before its "Needs …" rows, a round to resume "Unfinished: epoch 41" (detail "Narrowing round kept after epoch 41"), a round to continue "Unfinished: batch 236", marker `half`, `can_start` True. An offer's action is read from `(offer.kind, offer.step)`: `("resume", "training")` "resume" as today; `("round", "narrowing")` `"open_round"`; `("continue", "narrowing")` and `("fit_again", "narrowing")` `"continue_round"`. `_offered` for a kept round: heading "Next: resume the narrowing round retrain_round1", its sentence, button "Open Narrowing round", page "narrowing": the press opens the Narrowing round page with that kept round's row in sight and the keyboard ring on its Resume (`TSNPEPanel.show_kept(kept_id)`), and resumes nothing. The rail's step 6, pressed while a kept round is offered, opens the page the same way. A round's continue: "Next: continue the narrowing round", "Continue from batch 236". A kept TRAINING keeps stage 4's Resume on Home, unchanged. `Next.command` goes with its last caller.
- **The round page's list of kept rounds** (in region 2, read once at the triggers, never live): one row each: its label, `resume_sentence`, the line "Resume loads the posterior retrain_posterior, its prior retrain_prior and the observation cell_b, and puts them in use.", then "Resume" and "Delete"; a row that cannot be resumed: its `why_not`, "Delete" alone. "Delete": `ask`, "Delete this kept narrowing round?", "The 41 epochs kept are deleted; the next narrowing round with these settings starts its fit from the first epoch.", "Keep it", "Delete it", `loses_work=True`; `store.delete("fit", id)`; the list read again. The Training step's list keeps ordinary trainings only (departure 399), and `CONTROL["resume_fit"]`'s sentence stays Training's and true.
- **The Resume (departure 400).** `InferenceScreen.resume_round(kept) -> None`, from the round page's list and a past view, one path; never from Home. First, before anything is read, `resume.round_problems(kept, *, device, store) -> tuple[str, ...]`: `resume_problems`' sentences, and "The posterior it narrows, retrain_posterior, is no longer in Records." / "The observation it was trained around is no longer in Records." (by the ids the offers' reading already listed). Any: said on the row; nothing read. A name: `kept.posterior_name` unless another posterior has taken it since or it is empty, then `ask_text(self, "Name this narrowing round", "Its name, retrain_round1, has been taken by another posterior since it was kept. The posterior it makes needs another.", "Name", "Resume", check=<the name's check>)` (the empty case: "This kept narrowing round has no name yet. A posterior needs one."); Cancel starts nothing. Something held in the session: `ask`, "Resume this narrowing round?", naming what it lets go by `session_contents()`, "Keep this session", "Resume and start a new session". Then `new_draft(resume_draft(kept))`, `load_chain(model, options, bounds, kept.parents["prior"], posterior=kept.parents["parent_posterior"], observation=kept.parents["observation"], candidate=resume_config(kept))` (the bounds file's name through `resume.bounds_key`, as a training's Resume names it), and on the chain's `finished` `tsnpe_panel.resume(kept, name)`: `dispatch(orchestrator.tsnpe_round, s.cfg, s.posterior, s.inf_prior, s.observation, **resume.round_call(kept, name), if_name_taken="unnamed", provide_fig_sink=True, on_result=self._on_round)`; `round_call(kept, name=None) -> dict` is `{"resume_fit": kept.id}` and `name` only when asked for. Nothing the stage compares is handed (no `n_directions`, no `level`, no budget, network, cadence or note): probe 4's refusals (the level, the directions, another observation, another budget, checkpointing off, a name taken) are what a wrong hand-over meets. The spec takes patience, epoch limit, network and cadence from `kept.config`, `spec.name` the kept name. The page keeps its own record of the row pressed (`TSNPEPanel._resumed_row`, cleared when the run ends or the list is drawn again); a `resume_fit` refusal is said on that row, with "Delete", and the list read again.
- **Continue from batch N (C2, C4; 8.1).** `InferenceScreen.continue_round(offer) -> None`, as Training's `continue_from`: `load_chain(settings["model"], settings["options"], settings["bounds"], settings["prior"], posterior=settings["parent_posterior"], observation=settings["observation"], fill=<the fill>)`, the fill, after step 1 is applied, being `fill_forms(screen, values, PlanSource("run", <id>, …))` of the history's `settings["rows"]`, which reaches the round's form through `HEADING_OF["narrowing"]` (Task 12); then `tsnpe_panel.continue_run(offer)`: level and directions from the settings, "Name" and "Note" blank with "The earlier run was named retrain_round1." under "Name", the main button "Continue from batch 236" (N from the history's `simulation_set`, the set's own count) or "Fit again on the saved simulations", the next dispatch passing `resume="require"`; the simulations fact reads "Continues this round's simulation set from batch 236."; a region that came out different is the stage's refusal under `resume`, said on the page.
- **A new round over a kept one (probe 4).** Before "Start the narrowing round", a kept round whose `parents` (prior, parent posterior, observation), `region["level"]` and `config` (batches, simulations per batch, network) match the form's: `ask(self, *new_round_question(sentence))`, title "Start a new narrowing round beside a kept one?", text "The narrowing round retrain_round1 was kept after epoch 2 with these settings. Resume it from the list on this page to train on from there. A new round starts its fit from the first epoch, leaves the kept one as it is, and takes its name.", "Cancel" preselected, "Start a new round".
- **Words of a reopening (`retry`).** A round's failure page after which only a reopening helps: "<opening> Close PRISM and open it again; the Narrowing round step will offer 'Resume', and Home will lead there." No command is shown after this stage: `retry.reopen_command`, `resume_command`, `untold` and `_flags`, whose only caller was the reopening's command, go with it, and the test legs that call them go too (`tests/test_retry.py:1317-1324`, `tests/test_step_chain.py:160`).
